# Events

Every event code in embassy's list, then the codes other drivers define that embassy's list lacks and how each driver sizes its event mask.

### 0 SET_SSID
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | linux `BRCMF_E_SET_SSID` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:26` / whd `WLC_E_SET_SSID` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:45` and public header `whd/inc/whd_events.h:37` / c-driver `CYW43_EV_SET_SSID` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:50` / embassy `Event::SET_SSID` `embassy/events.rs:18`. Same stem in every driver; only the prefix differs. |
| Mask | linux enables it: a handler is registered `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:7009-7010` and the mask bit of every event with a handler is set `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-452`. whd enables it while its join handler is registered: the code is in `join_events` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160`, registered at `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:2069`; mask rebuilt from all registered lists `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-419`. embassy leaves it set in its default mask (24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG, ROAM cleared) `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. embassy also enables it in its own local subscription mask during join only (SET_SSID, AUTH, PSK_SUP) `embassy/control.rs:464`; cleared by `disable_all` on drop `embassy/control.rs:458`; the runner queues an event only if this local mask has it `embassy/runner.rs:1316`. c-driver leaves it set in its default mask (19 bytes of 0xff with only events 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared) `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`. c-driver has a commented-out (not compiled) narrower join-time mask that names this event `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2282-2294`. |
| Handled by | linux, whd, embassy, c-driver: `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6255-6263`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6303-6306`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6836-6842`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1405-1420`, `embassy/control.rs:480-484`, `embassy/control.rs:480`, `embassy/control.rs:487-489`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:355-365`. |
| Event data | no driver parses the data (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6255-6263`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6303-6306`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6836-6842`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1405-1420`, `embassy/control.rs:480-484`, `embassy/control.rs:480`, `embassy/control.rs:487-489`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:355-365`). |

- linux — `brcmf_is_linkup`: on status SUCCESS copies the event address into the profile BSSID; returns link-up at once unless the profile's `use_fwsup` is PSK or SAE, in which case it sets ASSOC_SUCCESS and waits for PSK_SUP — reads status, addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6255-6263`
- linux — `brcmf_is_nonetwork`: any status other than SUCCESS is treated as no network found and the connect attempt is reported failed — reads status — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6303-6306`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6836-6842`
- whd — join handler: SUCCESS sets JOIN_SSID_SET; NO_NETWORKS sets JOIN_NO_NETWORKS without ending the attempt; any other status ends the join attempt — reads status, bsscfgidx — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1405-1420`
- embassy — join loop: SUCCESS ends the join OK on an open network; NO_NETWORKS gives `JoinError::NetworkNotFound`; any other non-SUCCESS status gives `JoinError::JoinFailure(status)` — reads status — `embassy/control.rs:480-484`
- embassy — join loop on a secured network: SET_SSID SUCCESS is ignored and the loop waits for PSK_SUP — reads status — `embassy/control.rs:480`, `embassy/control.rs:487-489`
- c-driver — status 0 does nothing; status 3 with reason 0 sets join state NONET; anything else sets join state FAIL — reads status, reason — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:355-365`
- Values tested, linux: status == BRCMF_E_STATUS_SUCCESS (0) and status != SUCCESS `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6255`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6303`, `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:118`
- Values tested, whd: WLC_E_STATUS_SUCCESS (0), WLC_E_STATUS_NO_NETWORKS (3) `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1406`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1412`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:206`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:209`
- Values tested, embassy: EStatus::SUCCESS (0), EStatus::NO_NETWORKS (3) `embassy/control.rs:480-482`, `embassy/consts.rs:258`, `embassy/consts.rs:264`
- Values tested, c-driver: status 0; status 3 with reason 0 `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:356-358`
- Only linux reads the address field (as the joined BSSID) `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6257`
- Only c-driver requires reason == 0 for the no-network case `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:358`
- embassy publishes synthetic SET_SSID events (status NO_NETWORKS, FAIL, SUCCESS; reason 0) in its unit tests only `embassy/control.rs:1123`, `embassy/control.rs:1152`, `embassy/control.rs:971-983`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN_STRING`, so it returns the name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:215`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`
- whd `whd_event_info_to_string` maps cmd 0 to "WLC_E_SET_SSID" for its command log `whd/src/whd_utils.c:1086-1089`
- c-driver trace name table has an entry for this code `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:278`
- Mask transport, linux (all events): the mask goes out as the named variable (iovar) `event_msgs` on the generic path `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:462`, or inside `event_msgs_ext` when the vendor module supplies `activate_events`, in which case the generic write is skipped `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:459-460`, `linux/brcmfmac/fwvid.h:73-81`, `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:79-99`, `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:381`
- Mask transport, linux before activation (all events): preinit reads `event_msgs` back from the chip and only adds the IF bit, so until activation (called at `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:8637`) the mask linux read back, plus IF, stays in force (inferred); the content of that read-back mask is not found in these sources `linux/brcmfmac/common.c:414-428`
- Mask transport, whd (all events): 16 bytes (WL_EVENTING_MASK_LEN) sent as `bsscfg:event_msgs` with a 4-byte bsscfgidx prefix, or `bsscfg:event_msgs_ext` when any registered event code is above 127; at init whd first sets an all-zero `event_msgs` mask `whd/src/include/whd_wlioctl.h:1932`, `whd/src/whd_events.c:287-300`, `whd/src/whd_events.c:266-276`, `whd/src/whd_events.c:302-323`, `whd/src/whd_management.c:512-524`
- Mask transport, embassy and c-driver (all events): both send their mask as `bsscfg:event_msgs`. embassy's value is a 4-byte `iface` field (0) followed by the 24 mask bytes; c-driver's buffer is the 18-byte name (with its NUL), 4 bytes, then the 19 mask bytes, 41 bytes in all `embassy/control.rs:305-319`, `embassy/structs.rs:437-440`, `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`
- linux registrations elsewhere (all events): the `brcmf_fweh_register` calls outside `brcmf_register_event_handlers` name PSM_WATCHDOG, EXT_AUTH_REQ, EXT_AUTH_FRAME_RX, MGMT_FRAME_TXSTATUS, MGMT_FRAME_OFFCHAN_DONE, FIFO_CREDIT_MAP, BCMC_CREDIT_SUPPORT, ESCAN_RESULT, PFN_NET_FOUND and TDLS_PEER_EVENT; none is a code 0-17 `linux/brcmfmac/core.c:1399`, `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:363-369`, `linux/brcmfmac/fwsignal.c:2379-2385`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3787`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4232`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4279`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:8668`, `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:26-43`
- c-driver's commented-out join mask names SET_SSID 0, AUTH 3, DEAUTH_IND 6, DISASSOC_IND 12, LINK 16, PSK_SUP 46, ESCAN_RESULT 69, CSA_COMPLETE_IND 80; its first bytes 0x49 0x10 0x01 set bits 0, 3, 6, 12, 16 (bit positions inferred from the literal); the live code keeps the enable-all mask from init `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2282-2294`
- Header byte order (all events): header fields, not event data, are converted from big-endian by linux `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:288-294`, whd `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:843-848`, c-driver (flags, event_type, status, reason only) `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:738-741`, embassy (EventPacket byteswap) `embassy/structs.rs:361-364`
- embassy truncates event_type and status to u8 before matching (all events) `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1229`
- Header layout (all events): linux's event message struct has addr at offset 24, ifname at 30, ifidx at 46, bsscfgidx at 47, size 48 (offsets computed from the field sizes, taking `ETH_ALEN` = 6 and `IFNAMSIZ` = 16; neither constant is defined in these sources, so the linux offsets and size depend on those values; whd's and embassy's parallel fields are a 6-byte address and an explicit 16-byte name) `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:265-277` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:444` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:492-493` `whd/inc/whd_types.h:553` `embassy/structs.rs:265-267`
- Header layout, c-driver (all events): its `interface` byte is at offset 46 of its event struct; the struct starts at the event message because the parser passes payload + 24, past the 14-byte ethernet and 10-byte event headers; the event parser then moves the bytes down by 2 for alignment and casts `&buf[-2]` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:237-245`, `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:1080-1082`, `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:720-735`

### 1 JOIN
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | linux `BRCMF_E_JOIN` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:27` / whd `WLC_E_JOIN` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:46` (internal header only; not found in `whd/inc/whd_events.h`) / c-driver `CYW43_EV_JOIN` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:51` / embassy `Event::JOIN` `embassy/events.rs:20`. Same stem in every driver; only the prefix differs. |
| Mask | linux leaves the bit 0: no `brcmf_fweh_register` call for this code `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`; the mask is zeroed and only handler-backed events are set `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-452`; an arriving event with no handler is dropped `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499`. whd does not enable it itself: the code is in neither `join_events` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` nor `apsta_events` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70`; the bit is set only if an application registers the event with `whd_wifi_set_event_handler` `whd/src/whd_events.c:443`, `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-419`. embassy leaves it set in its default mask (24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG, ROAM cleared) `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. c-driver leaves it set in its default mask (19 bytes of 0xff with only events 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared) `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`. |
| Handled by | embassy `embassy/runner.rs:1268-1271`. No handler: linux (no registration `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`); whd (unregistered-event case list `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1481`); c-driver (no branch `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`). |
| Event data | no driver parses the data (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1481`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`). |

- embassy — runner: JOIN with status SUCCESS sets `join_ok` and recomputes link state (Up if `join_ok` and, on a secured network, `key_exchange_ok`) — reads status — `embassy/runner.rs:1268-1271`, `embassy/runner.rs:1296-1304`
- whd — not handled: the join handler lists this code only in its case list that falls to the "not registered" assert — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1481`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615`
- c-driver — name table entry "JOIN" for the trace dump only; no branch tests the code — reads no fields — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:279`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`
- Values tested, embassy: status == EStatus::SUCCESS (0) `embassy/runner.rs:1268`, `embassy/consts.rs:258`
- No other driver tests a status or reason for this event `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1481`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`
- embassy's runner match runs for every received event; the local subscription mask is checked later `embassy/runner.rs:1237`, `embassy/runner.rs:1316`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks and returns no name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:217`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`

### 2 START
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | linux `BRCMF_E_START` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:28` / whd `WLC_E_START` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:47` (internal header only; not found in `whd/inc/whd_events.h`) / c-driver: no define for this code `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:50-66` / embassy `Event::START` `embassy/events.rs:22`. Same stem in every driver that defines it; only the prefix differs. |
| Mask | linux leaves the bit 0: no `brcmf_fweh_register` call for this code `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`; the mask is zeroed and only handler-backed events are set `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-452`; an arriving event with no handler is dropped `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499`. whd does not enable it itself: the code is in neither `join_events` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` nor `apsta_events` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70`; the bit is set only if an application registers the event with `whd_wifi_set_event_handler` `whd/src/whd_events.c:443`, `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-419`. embassy leaves it set in its default mask (24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG, ROAM cleared) `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. c-driver leaves it set in its default mask (19 bytes of 0xff with only events 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared) `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`. |
| Handled by | no driver in these sources handles it: linux (no registration `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`); embassy (no match arm `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`); c-driver (no branch `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`); whd (unregistered-event case list `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1482`). |
| Event data | no driver parses the data (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`, `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1482`). |

- whd — not handled: the join handler lists this code only in its case list that falls to the "not registered" assert — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1482`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615`
- No status or reason value is tested by any driver `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`, `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`
- Name comments in embassy and whd describe it as an IBSS founded or an AP BSS started `embassy/events.rs:21`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:47`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks and returns no name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:218`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`

### 3 AUTH
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | linux `BRCMF_E_AUTH` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:29` / whd `WLC_E_AUTH` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:48` and public header `whd/inc/whd_events.h:38` / c-driver `CYW43_EV_AUTH` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:52` / embassy `Event::AUTH` `embassy/events.rs:24`. Same stem in every driver; only the prefix differs. |
| Mask | linux leaves the bit 0: no `brcmf_fweh_register` call for this code `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`; the mask is zeroed and only handler-backed events are set `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-452`; an arriving event with no handler is dropped `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499`. whd enables it while its join handler is registered: the code is in `join_events` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160`, registered at `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:2069`; mask rebuilt from all registered lists `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-419`. embassy leaves it set in its default mask (24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG, ROAM cleared) `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. embassy also enables it in its own local subscription mask during join only (SET_SSID, AUTH, PSK_SUP) `embassy/control.rs:464`; cleared by `disable_all` on drop `embassy/control.rs:458`; the runner queues an event only if this local mask has it `embassy/runner.rs:1316`. c-driver leaves it set in its default mask (19 bytes of 0xff with only events 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared) `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`. c-driver has a commented-out (not compiled) narrower join-time mask that names this event `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2282-2294`. |
| Handled by | whd `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1439-1453`, embassy `embassy/runner.rs:1253-1266`, `embassy/control.rs:491-493`, c-driver `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:366-382`. No handler: linux (no registration `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`). |
| Event data | no driver parses the data. The `auth_type` field tested by embassy is a header field, not event data `embassy/structs.rs:260-261`. |

- whd — join handler: SUCCESS sets JOIN_AUTHENTICATED; UNSOLICITED is ignored; any other status ends the join attempt — reads status, bsscfgidx — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1439-1453`
- embassy — runner, matched first: status FAIL with reason 16 and auth_type 3 (commented as specific to WPA3) clears `auth_ok`, `join_ok`, `key_exchange_ok` and sets link Down — reads status, reason, auth_type — `embassy/runner.rs:1253-1258`
- embassy — runner: any other AUTH whose status is not UNSOLICITED sets `auth_ok` = (status == SUCCESS); no link state update — reads status — `embassy/runner.rs:1262-1266`
- embassy — join loop: status FAIL on a secured network ends the join with `JoinError::AuthenticationFailure` — reads status — `embassy/control.rs:491-493`
- c-driver — status 0 sets WIFI_JOIN_STATE_AUTH (and moves BADAUTH back to ACTIVE); status 6, 2 or 5 is ignored; anything else sets join state BADAUTH — reads status — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:366-382`
- Values tested, whd: WLC_E_STATUS_SUCCESS (0), WLC_E_STATUS_UNSOLICITED (6) `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1440`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1444`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:206`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:212`
- Values tested, embassy: status FAIL (1) with reason 16 and auth_type 3; status != UNSOLICITED (6); status == SUCCESS (0); status FAIL `embassy/runner.rs:1253`, `embassy/runner.rs:1262-1263`, `embassy/control.rs:491`, `embassy/consts.rs:258-270`
- Values tested, c-driver: status 0; status 6 (unsolicited), 2 (timeout), 5 (no ack) ignored `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:367`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:373-376`
- Drivers disagree on timeout and no-ack: c-driver ignores status 2 and 5 `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:373-376`; whd ends the join on any status other than SUCCESS or UNSOLICITED `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1448-1452`
- The event header has an `auth_type` field, commented "WLC_E_AUTH" in embassy and whd `embassy/structs.rs:260-261`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:490`, `whd/inc/whd_events.h:167`, `whd/src/whd_sdpcm.c:124`
- linux declares `auth_type` as s32, documented only as "authentication type" `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:258`, `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:271`
- Only embassy tests `auth_type`; linux and whd byte-swap it but no handler for this event reads it `embassy/runner.rs:1253`, `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:293`, `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:847`
- c-driver's event struct does not expose `auth_type`; it lies inside the `_1[30]` padding `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:237-245`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN_STRING`, so it returns the name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:219`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`
- whd `whd_event_info_to_string` maps cmd 3 to "WLC_E_AUTH" `whd/src/whd_utils.c:1090-1093`
- c-driver trace name table has an entry for this code `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:280`

### 5 DEAUTH
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | linux `BRCMF_E_DEAUTH` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:31` / whd `WLC_E_DEAUTH` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:50` and public header `whd/inc/whd_events.h:39` / c-driver `CYW43_EV_DEAUTH` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:53` / embassy `Event::DEAUTH` `embassy/events.rs:28`. Same stem in every driver; only the prefix differs. |
| Mask | linux enables it: a handler is registered `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6997-6998` and the mask bit of every event with a handler is set `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-452`. whd does not enable it itself: the code is in neither `join_events` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` nor `apsta_events` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70`; the bit is set only if an application registers the event with `whd_wifi_set_event_handler` `whd/src/whd_events.c:443`, `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-419`. embassy leaves it set in its default mask (24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG, ROAM cleared) `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. c-driver leaves it set in its default mask (19 bytes of 0xff with only events 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared) `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`. |
| Handled by | linux `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6789-6794`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6771-6775`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, embassy `embassy/runner.rs:1250`; whd rewrites status and reason at dispatch `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:876-880` but has no internal handler `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1484`. No handler: c-driver (defined only; not in its name table `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:276-292`; no branch `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`). |
| Event data | no driver parses the data (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6789-6794`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6771-6775`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, `embassy/runner.rs:1250`, `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:876-880`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1484`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`). |

- linux — `brcmf_notify_connect_status`: deletes the peer for the event address (`brcmf_proto_delete_peer`) — reads addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6789-6794`
- linux — AP mode: `cfg80211_del_sta` for the event address — reads addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6771-6775`
- linux — STA mode: counts as link down; ignored if connected and the event address differs from the profile BSSID; otherwise reports connect failure, calls `brcmf_link_down` with the event's reason, clears the profile, drops carrier — reads addr, reason — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6280-6288`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:1696-1700`
- embassy — runner: DEAUTH with status SUCCESS clears `auth_ok`, `join_ok`, `key_exchange_ok` and sets link Down — reads status — `embassy/runner.rs:1250`, `embassy/runner.rs:1254-1257`
- whd — dispatch only: before calling any registered handler, adds WLC_DOT11_SC_STATUS_OFFSET (512) to status and WLC_E_DOT11_RC_REASON_OFFSET (768) to reason — rewrites status, reason — `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:876-880`, `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:832-836`
- Values tested, embassy: status == EStatus::SUCCESS (0) `embassy/runner.rs:1250`
- Values tested, linux: no status test; reason is passed through as the 802.11 reason code `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:1697-1700`
- Values, whd: application handlers see offset values, e.g. WLC_DOT11_SC_SUCCESS = 512; offsets defined at `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:198`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:268`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:237`
- linux computes "locally generated" as NOT (event_code AND (BRCMF_E_DEAUTH_IND OR BRCMF_E_DISASSOC_IND)), a bitwise AND of event codes with 14; 5 AND 14 = 4, so DEAUTH is reported as not locally generated (inferred from the expression) `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6827-6830`
- whd's public header comment ties DEAUTH to stopping the SoftAP to deauthenticate connected stations `whd/inc/whd_events.h:39`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks and returns no name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:221`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`

- linux — `brcmf_notify_connect_status`: deletes the peer for the event address — reads addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6789-6794`
- linux — AP mode: `cfg80211_del_sta` for the event address — reads addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6771-6775`
- linux — STA mode: link down; ignored if connected and addr differs from the profile BSSID; otherwise connect failure reported and `brcmf_link_down` called with the event's reason — reads addr, reason — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6280-6288`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:1696-1700`
- whd — join handler: clears JOIN_AUTHENTICATED and JOIN_LINK_READY for the bsscfg — reads bsscfgidx — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1433-1437`
- embassy — runner: any DEAUTH_IND, whatever its status or reason, clears `auth_ok`, `join_ok`, `key_exchange_ok` and sets link Down — reads no field beyond the event type — `embassy/runner.rs:1251`, `embassy/runner.rs:1254-1257`
- c-driver — status 0 with reason 2 sets `pend_disassoc` and schedules the poll function (source comment: probably a wrong password; disassociate) — reads status, reason — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:383-388`
- Values tested, c-driver: status == 0 and reason == 2 `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:384`
- Values tested, linux: none; reason is passed through as the 802.11 reason code `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:1698-1700`
- embassy and whd test no status or reason for this event `embassy/runner.rs:1251`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1433-1437`
- whd does not apply the 512/768 status/reason offsets here; the offset branch names only WLC_E_DISASSOC and WLC_E_DEAUTH `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:876`
- linux: 6 AND 14 is nonzero, so `brcmf_link_down` gets locally_generated = false (inferred from the expression) `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6827-6830`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN_STRING`, so it returns the name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:222`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`
- c-driver trace name table has an entry for this code `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:281`

| Handled by | no driver in these sources handles it: linux (no registration `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`); embassy (no match arm `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`); c-driver (no branch `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`); whd (unregistered-event case list `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1485`). |
| Event data | no driver parses the data (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`, `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1485`). |

- whd — not handled: the join handler lists this code only in its case list that falls to the "not registered" assert — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1485`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615`
- c-driver — name table entry "ASSOC" for the trace dump only; no branch tests the code — reads no fields — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:282`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`
- No status or reason value is tested by any driver `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`, `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`
- whd's public header comment: occurs when a STA joins the AP `whd/inc/whd_events.h:41`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks and returns no name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:223`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`

- linux in STA mode: the event passes `brcmf_is_linkup`, `brcmf_is_linkdown` and `brcmf_is_nonetwork` without matching `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6796-6842`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks and returns no name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:224`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`

|---|---|
| Names | linux `BRCMF_E_DISASSOC_IND` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:38` / whd `WLC_E_DISASSOC_IND` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:57` and public header `whd/inc/whd_events.h:46` / c-driver `CYW43_EV_DISASSOC_IND` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:57` / embassy `Event::DISASSOC_IND` `embassy/events.rs:42`. Same stem in every driver; only the prefix differs. |
| Mask | linux enables it: a handler is registered `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6999-7000` and the mask bit of every event with a handler is set `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-452`. whd enables it while its join handler is registered: the code is in `join_events` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160`, registered at `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:2069`; mask rebuilt from all registered lists `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-419`. embassy leaves it set in its default mask (24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG, ROAM cleared) `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. c-driver leaves it set in its default mask (19 bytes of 0xff with only events 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared) `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`. c-driver has a commented-out (not compiled) narrower join-time mask that names this event `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2282-2294`. |
| Handled by | linux `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6789-6794`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6771-6775`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, whd `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1433-1437`, embassy `embassy/runner.rs:1252`. c-driver has a branch but it is compiled out with `#if 0` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:339-345`. |
| Event data | no driver parses the data (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6789-6794`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6771-6775`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1433-1437`, `embassy/runner.rs:1252`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:339-345`). |

- linux — `brcmf_notify_connect_status`: deletes the peer for the event address — reads addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6789-6794`
- linux — AP mode: `cfg80211_del_sta` for the event address — reads addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6771-6775`
- linux — STA mode: link down; ignored if connected and addr differs from the profile BSSID; otherwise connect failure reported and `brcmf_link_down` called with the event's reason — reads addr, reason — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6280-6288`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:1696-1700`
- whd — join handler: clears JOIN_AUTHENTICATED and JOIN_LINK_READY for the bsscfg — reads bsscfgidx — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1433-1437`
- whd — msgbuf bus only, under CERT_MULTI_AKM and COMPONENT_CAT5, when `cert_mbssid_enable` is set: rewrites the event into WLC_E_LINK with flags 0 and reason WLC_E_LINK_BCN_LOSS before calling the handler — rewrites event_type, flags, reason — `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:868-884`
- embassy — runner: any DISASSOC_IND, whatever its status or reason, clears `auth_ok`, `join_ok`, `key_exchange_ok` and sets link Down — reads no field beyond the event type — `embassy/runner.rs:1252`, `embassy/runner.rs:1254-1257`
- c-driver — dead code (`#if 0`): for the AP interface it would have told a DHCP server that a station left, passing `buf + 24` — reads nothing in the compiled driver — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:339-345`
- No driver tests a status or reason value for this event `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6280-6288`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1433-1437`, `embassy/runner.rs:1252`
- linux passes reason through as the 802.11 reason code `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:1699-1700`
- whd's conditional rewrite sets reason = WLC_E_LINK_BCN_LOSS (1) `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:881`, `whd/inc/whd_events.h:128`
- whd does not apply the 512/768 offsets here; only DISASSOC and DEAUTH get them `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:876`
- linux: 12 AND 14 is nonzero, so locally_generated = false (inferred from the expression) `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6827-6830`
- embassy's comment calls it an AP-initiated disassoc, e.g. an idle-station timeout `embassy/runner.rs:1248`
- The dead c-driver code uses `buf`, but no variable of that name exists in the function, so the buffer meant is not found in these sources; 24 would equal the addr offset if `buf` pointed at the event message (inferred) `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-343`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN_STRING`, so it returns the name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:228`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`
- c-driver trace name table has an entry for this code `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:284`

### 13 QUIET_START
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | linux `BRCMF_E_QUIET_START` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:39` / whd `WLC_E_QUIET_START` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:58` (internal header only; not found in `whd/inc/whd_events.h`) / c-driver: no define for this code `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:50-66` / embassy `Event::QUIET_START` `embassy/events.rs:44`. Same stem in every driver that defines it; only the prefix differs. |
| Mask | linux leaves the bit 0: no `brcmf_fweh_register` call for this code `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`; the mask is zeroed and only handler-backed events are set `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-452`; an arriving event with no handler is dropped `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499`. whd does not enable it itself: the code is in neither `join_events` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` nor `apsta_events` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70`; the bit is set only if an application registers the event with `whd_wifi_set_event_handler` `whd/src/whd_events.c:443`, `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-419`. embassy leaves it set in its default mask (24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG, ROAM cleared) `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. c-driver leaves it set in its default mask (19 bytes of 0xff with only events 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared) `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`. |
| Handled by | no driver in these sources handles it: linux (no registration `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`); embassy (no match arm `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`); c-driver (no branch `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`); whd (unregistered-event case list `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1489`). |
| Event data | no driver parses the data (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`, `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1489`). |

- whd — not handled: the join handler lists this code only in its case list that falls to the "not registered" assert — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1489`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615`
- No status or reason value is tested by any driver `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6991-7030`, `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`
- Name comment in embassy and whd: 802.11h quiet period started `embassy/events.rs:43`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:58`
- embassy and c-driver leave the bit set in their masks and have no handler, so an arriving event is dropped without action (inferred) `embassy/control.rs:305-319`, `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`, `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-427`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks and returns no name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:232`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`

### 16 LINK
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | linux `BRCMF_E_LINK` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:42` / whd `WLC_E_LINK` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:61` and public header `whd/inc/whd_events.h:48` / c-driver `CYW43_EV_LINK` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:58` / embassy `Event::LINK` `embassy/events.rs:50`. Same stem in every driver; only the prefix differs. |
| Mask | linux enables it: a handler is registered `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6993-6994` and the mask bit of every event with a handler is set `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-452`. whd enables it while either internal list is registered: `join_events` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` (registered at `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:2069`) and `apsta_events` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70` (registered at `whd/src/whd_ap.c:280`); mask rebuilt from all registered lists `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-419`. embassy leaves it set in its default mask (24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG, ROAM cleared) `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. c-driver leaves it set in its default mask (19 bytes of 0xff with only events 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared) `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`. c-driver has a commented-out (not compiled) narrower join-time mask that names this event `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2282-2294`. |
| Handled by | linux, whd (join handler and AP handler), embassy, c-driver: `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6792-6794`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6745-6750`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6282-6288`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:1702-1705`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6297-6301`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6836-6842`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1422-1431`, `whd/src/whd_ap.c:167-191`, `embassy/runner.rs:1249`, `embassy/runner.rs:1254-1257`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:389-402`. |
| Event data | no driver parses the data (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6792-6794`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6745-6750`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6282-6288`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:1702-1705`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6297-6301`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6836-6842`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1422-1431`, `whd/src/whd_ap.c:167-191`, `embassy/runner.rs:1249`, `embassy/runner.rs:1254-1257`, `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:389-402`). |

- linux — `brcmf_notify_connect_status`: LINK with the whole flags field 0 deletes the peer for the event address — reads flags, addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6792-6794`
- linux — AP mode: LINK with reason BRCMF_E_REASON_LINK_BSSCFG_DIS on a non-primary netdev completes `cfg->vif_disabled` — reads reason — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6745-6750`
- linux — STA mode: LINK without the BRCMF_EVENT_MSG_LINK flag bit is link down; the reason handed to `brcmf_link_down` is mapped to 0 — reads flags, addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6282-6288`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6812-6835`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:1702-1705`
- linux — STA mode: LINK with status NO_NETWORKS is treated as no network found and the connect attempt is reported failed — reads status, flags (debug print only) — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6297-6301`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6836-6842`
- whd — join handler: sets JOIN_LINK_READY when flags has WLC_EVENT_MSG_LINK, clears it otherwise — reads flags, bsscfgidx — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1422-1431`
- whd — AP handler: while waiting for an AP event, a LINK (or IF) event posts the `whd_wifi_sleep_flag` semaphore — reads event_type only — `whd/src/whd_ap.c:167-191`
- embassy — runner: LINK with status SUCCESS and flags == 0 clears `auth_ok`, `join_ok`, `key_exchange_ok` and sets link Down; LINK with the link flag set is not acted on — reads status, flags — `embassy/runner.rs:1249`, `embassy/runner.rs:1254-1257`
- c-driver — status 0 only: flags bit 0 set is link up (STA interface: sets WIFI_JOIN_STATE_LINK; other interface: TCP/IP link up); bit 0 clear is TCP/IP link down for that interface — reads status, flags, interface — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:389-402`
- Flags bit 0x01 means link up for linux: BRCMF_EVENT_MSG_LINK 0x01 `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:113`, tested at `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6282`
- Flags bit 0x01 means link up for whd: WLC_EVENT_MSG_LINK 0x01 `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:58`, tested at `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1423`
- Flags bit 0x01 means link up for c-driver: `ev->flags & 1` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:391`
- Two link-down tests exist: bit 0x01 clear (linux `brcmf_is_linkdown`, whd, c-driver) versus the whole flags field equal to 0 (linux peer delete, embassy) `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6282`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6792`, `embassy/runner.rs:1249`
- Status tested: BRCMF_E_STATUS_NO_NETWORKS (3) by linux `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6297`, `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:121`; status SUCCESS (0) required by embassy `embassy/runner.rs:1249` and c-driver `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:390`
- Reason tested: BRCMF_E_REASON_LINK_BSSCFG_DIS (4) by linux `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6745`, `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:154`
- whd public reason names for LINK: WLC_E_LINK_BCN_LOSS 1, WLC_E_LINK_DISASSOC 2, WLC_E_LINK_ASSOC_REC 3, WLC_E_LINK_BSSCFG_DIS 4; whd tests none of them on receipt `whd/inc/whd_events.h:128-131`
- embassy comment, not code: reason 1 is loss of signal, reason 2 is a controlled network shutdown `embassy/runner.rs:1245`
- Other flag bits defined by linux: FLUSHTXQ 0x02, GROUP 0x04 `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:114-115`
- whd flag definitions: LINK 0x01, FLUSHTXQ 0x02, GROUP 0x04, UNKBSS 0x08, UNKIF 0x10 `whd/src/whd_sdpcm.c:54-58`; WLC_EVENT_MSG_LINK is also defined locally at `whd/src/whd_ap.c:44`
- linux does not test LINK with the link bit set as a link-up condition: `brcmf_is_linkup` keys on SET_SSID and PSK_SUP, although any event reaching it returns true once both EAP_SUCCESS and ASSOC_SUCCESS bits are already set `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6250-6271`
- linux: 16 AND 14 = 0, so a LINK link-down is reported as locally generated (inferred from the expression) `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6827-6830`
- whd can synthesise a LINK event from DISASSOC_IND under a certification build option `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:868-884`
- whd dispatch matches a handler by event type and ifidx `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:912-913`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN_STRING`, so it returns the name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:235`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`
- whd `whd_event_info_to_string` maps cmd 16 to "WLC_E_LINK" `whd/src/whd_utils.c:1094-1097`
- c-driver trace name table has an entry for this code `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:285`

- linux — `brcmf_notify_mic_status`: reports a Michael MIC failure to cfg80211 for the event address; key type is GROUP if flags has BRCMF_EVENT_MSG_GROUP, else PAIRWISE — reads flags, addr — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6868-6884`
- whd — not handled: the join handler lists this code only in its case list that falls to the "not registered" assert — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1492`, `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615`
- Values tested, linux: flags AND BRCMF_EVENT_MSG_GROUP (0x04); no status or reason `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6875`, `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:115`
- whd defines but never tests the matching flags: WLC_EVENT_MSG_FLUSHTXQ 0x02, WLC_EVENT_MSG_GROUP 0x04 `whd/src/whd_sdpcm.c:55-56`
- Name comment in embassy and whd: TKIP MIC error occurred `embassy/events.rs:51`, `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:62`
- whd `whd_event_to_string` lists this code with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks and returns no name `whd/src/whd_utils.c:237`, `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-197`

### 18 NDIS_LINK
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | embassy `NDIS_LINK` `embassy/events.rs:54` / linux `BRCMF_E_NDIS_LINK` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:44` / whd `WLC_E_NDIS_LINK` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:63` (internal header only; no define in the public header `whd/inc/whd_events.h:35-58`) / c-driver: no `CYW43_EV_` define for this code `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:50-66`. Spelling after the prefix is identical in all drivers that define it. |
| Mask | embassy: enabled by default. The 24-byte mask starts all-ones, this event is not among the `unset()` calls, and the mask is sent as named variable (iovar) `bsscfg:event_msgs` `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. Not enabled in the host-side event mask, which only gets SET_SSID/AUTH/PSK_SUP and ESCAN_RESULT `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`<br>c-driver: enabled by default. A 19-byte all-ones mask with only 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared is sent as `bsscfg:event_msgs` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`<br>linux: not enabled. The mask is zeroed and a bit is set only for events with a registered handler plus BRCMF_E_IF; no handler is registered for this event `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-462`. With the Cypress vendor ops the same mask bytes go out as named variable `event_msgs_ext` and `event_msgs` is skipped `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:79-99` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:459-460`<br>whd: left to the registered handler lists. The mask is zeroed and a bit set only for events in registered lists `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-416`; none of the driver's own lists contains this event `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70` `whd/src/COMPONENT_WLANSENSE/whd_wlansense_core.c:44-46`. An application can register for it `whd/src/whd_events.c:348` `whd/src/whd_events.c:443` |
| Handled by | no driver in these sources handles it `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` |
| Event data | no driver parses the data `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` |

- whd header comment: "NDIS style link indication"; embassy carries the same doc comment. `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:63` `embassy/events.rs:53`
- embassy — generic path only: converts the code with `as u8`, matches no link-state arm, and publishes `Status{event_type,status,reason}` only if the host-side mask has the event enabled, which no embassy code does for this event; the event is dropped after the debug log — reads event_type, status, flags, reason, auth_type in the match, data only logged — `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`
- whd — `case` label in `whd_wifi_join_events_handler` with no code of its own; it falls through to `default`, which only asserts ("Received event which was not registered"). The event is not in `join_events`, and the receive path calls a handler only for events in its registered list, so the label is not reached — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1493` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1477` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:906-925` `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:865`
- whd — `whd_event_to_string` lists it with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks, so the function returns "Unknown"; no caller of the function found in whd — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_utils.c:238` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:196-197` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:358` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:199` `whd/src/include/whd_utils.h:132`
- linux — no handler registered; an event without a handler is dropped in `brcmf_fweh_process_event` before it is queued — reads no fields — `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499`
- c-driver — `cyw43_cb_process_async_event` has no branch for this event; the optional trace prints the code as a number — reads no fields — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:299-304`
- No status or reason value is tested by any driver. `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166`
- pico-sdk — no reference to this event: not found in these sources.

### 19 ROAM
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | embassy `ROAM` `embassy/events.rs:56` / linux `BRCMF_E_ROAM` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:45` / whd `WLC_E_ROAM` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:64` (internal header only; no define in the public header `whd/inc/whd_events.h:35-58`) / c-driver `CYW43_EV_ROAM` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:59`. Spelling after the prefix is identical in all drivers that define it. |
| Mask | embassy: explicitly disabled. `evts.unset(Event::ROAM)` clears bit 19 of the all-ones mask sent as `bsscfg:event_msgs` `embassy/control.rs:305-308` `embassy/control.rs:317` `embassy/control.rs:319`<br>c-driver: enabled by default. A 19-byte all-ones mask with only 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared is sent as `bsscfg:event_msgs` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`<br>linux: enabled. A handler is registered `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:7005` and every event with a handler gets its mask bit, sent as named variable `event_msgs` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-462`. With the Cypress vendor ops the same mask bytes go out as named variable `event_msgs_ext` and `event_msgs` is skipped `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:79-99` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:459-460`<br>whd: left to the registered handler lists. The mask is zeroed and a bit set only for events in registered lists `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-416`; none of the driver's own lists contains this event `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70` `whd/src/COMPONENT_WLANSENSE/whd_wlansense_core.c:44-46`. An application can register for it `whd/src/whd_events.c:348` `whd/src/whd_events.c:443` |
| Handled by | linux (`brcmf_notify_roaming_status`) `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6848-6866`. c-driver has a debug name only `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:286`. embassy and whd: no handler `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166`. |
| Event data | no driver parses the data `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6848-6866` `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` |

- whd header comment: "roam attempt occurred: indicate status & reason"; embassy carries the same doc comment. `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:64` `embassy/events.rs:55`
- linux — `brcmf_notify_roaming_status`: on ROAM with status SUCCESS, calls `brcmf_bss_roaming_done` if the vif is already CONNECTED, otherwise `brcmf_bss_connect_done(..., true)` and sets carrier on; any other status is ignored — reads event_code and status; addr is copied as the new BSSID in `brcmf_bss_roaming_done`; data not read — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6848-6866` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6612` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:7005`
- linux tests status == `BRCMF_E_STATUS_SUCCESS` (0). No driver tests the reason. `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:6855` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:118`
- c-driver — debug naming only: entry "ROAM" in `cyw43_async_event_name_table`, printed by `cyw43_dump_async_event` when `CYW43_TRACE_ASYNC_EV` is set; `cyw43_cb_process_async_event` has no branch for `CYW43_EV_ROAM` — prints flags, event_type, status, reason, interface — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:286` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:294-310` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:315-316`
- embassy — generic path only: converts the code with `as u8`, matches no link-state arm, and publishes `Status{event_type,status,reason}` only if the host-side mask has the event enabled, which no embassy code does for this event; the event is dropped after the debug log — reads event_type, status, flags, reason, auth_type in the match, data only logged — `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`
- whd — `case` label in `whd_wifi_join_events_handler` with no code of its own; it falls through to `default`, which only asserts ("Received event which was not registered"). The event is not in `join_events`, and the receive path calls a handler only for events in its registered list, so the label is not reached — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1480` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1477` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:906-925` `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:865`
- whd — `whd_event_to_string` lists it with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks, so the function returns "Unknown"; no caller of the function found in whd — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_utils.c:229` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:196-197` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:358` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:199` `whd/src/include/whd_utils.h:132`
- Reason codes are defined but not tested for this event. whd, under the comment "roam reason codes": `WLC_E_REASON_` INITIAL_ASSOC 0, LOW_RSSI 1, DEAUTH 2, DISASSOC 3, BCNS_LOST 4, FAST_ROAM_FAILED 5, DIRECTED_ROAM 6, TSPEC_REJECTED 7, BETTER_AP 8 `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:277-286`. c-driver: `CYW43_REASON_INITIAL_ASSOC`..`CYW43_REASON_BETTER_AP`, 0..8, with "roamed due to" line comments under a heading that reads "Values for AP auth setting" `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:98-107`. linux, under "reason field values" and not labelled as roam reasons: `BRCMF_E_REASON_` INITIAL_ASSOC 0, LOW_RSSI 1, DEAUTH 2, DISASSOC 3, BCNS_LOST 4, MINTXRATE 9, TXFAIL 10, and FAST_ROAM_FAILED 5, DIRECTED_ROAM 6, TSPEC_REJECTED 7, BETTER_AP 8 `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:145-152` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:155-158`; linux also defines `BRCMF_E_REASON_LINK_BSSCFG_DIS` 4 in the same block `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:154`
- linux `BRCMF_E_REASON_TXFAIL` (10) is a reason value, not the TXFAIL event (code 20). `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:152`
- pico-sdk — no reference to this event: not found in these sources.

| Event data | no driver parses the data `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` |

- whd header comment: "current AP's TSF value went backward"; embassy carries the same doc comment. `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:67` `embassy/events.rs:61`
- embassy — generic path only: converts the code with `as u8`, matches no link-state arm, and publishes `Status{event_type,status,reason}` only if the host-side mask has the event enabled, which no embassy code does for this event; the event is dropped after the debug log — reads event_type, status, flags, reason, auth_type in the match, data only logged — `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`
- whd — `case` label in `whd_wifi_join_events_handler` with no code of its own; it falls through to `default`, which only asserts ("Received event which was not registered"). The event is not in `join_events`, and the receive path calls a handler only for events in its registered list, so the label is not reached — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1496` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1477` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:906-925` `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:865`
- whd — `whd_event_to_string` lists it with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks, so the function returns "Unknown"; no caller of the function found in whd — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_utils.c:241` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:196-197` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:358` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:199` `whd/src/include/whd_utils.h:132`
- linux — no handler registered; an event without a handler is dropped in `brcmf_fweh_process_event` before it is queued — reads no fields — `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499`
- c-driver — `cyw43_cb_process_async_event` has no branch for this event; the optional trace prints the code as a number — reads no fields — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:299-304`
- No status or reason value is tested by any driver. `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166`
- pico-sdk — no reference to this event: not found in these sources.

### 23 PRUNE
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | embassy `PRUNE` `embassy/events.rs:64` / linux `BRCMF_E_PRUNE` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:49` / whd `WLC_E_PRUNE` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:68` (internal header only; no define in the public header `whd/inc/whd_events.h:35-58`) / c-driver `CYW43_EV_PRUNE` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:60`. Spelling after the prefix is identical in all drivers that define it. |
| Mask | embassy: enabled by default. The 24-byte mask starts all-ones, this event is not among the `unset()` calls, and the mask is sent as named variable `bsscfg:event_msgs` `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. Not enabled in the host-side event mask, which only gets SET_SSID/AUTH/PSK_SUP and ESCAN_RESULT `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`<br>c-driver: enabled by default. A 19-byte all-ones mask with only 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared is sent as `bsscfg:event_msgs` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`<br>linux: not enabled. The mask is zeroed and a bit is set only for events with a registered handler plus BRCMF_E_IF; no handler is registered for this event `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-462`. With the Cypress vendor ops the same mask bytes go out as named variable `event_msgs_ext` and `event_msgs` is skipped `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:79-99` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:459-460`<br>whd: left to the registered handler lists. The mask is zeroed and a bit set only for events in registered lists `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-416`; none of the driver's own lists contains this event `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70` `whd/src/COMPONENT_WLANSENSE/whd_wlansense_core.c:44-46`. An application can register for it `whd/src/whd_events.c:348` `whd/src/whd_events.c:443` |
| Handled by | c-driver (`cyw43_cb_process_async_event`) `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:348-354`. whd adjusts the reason in the receive path but has no handler `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:872-875`. embassy and linux: no handler `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499`. |
| Event data | no driver parses the data `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:348-354` `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:872-875` `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499` |

- whd header comment: "AP was pruned from join list for reason"; embassy carries the same doc comment. `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:68` `embassy/events.rs:63`
- c-driver — on PRUNE with status 0 and reason 8, sets `pend_rejoin` and `pend_rejoin_wpa` and schedules the poll; the poll sets WPA auth to `CYW43_WPA_AUTH_PSK` with WLC_SET_WPA_AUTH and re-issues WLC_SET_SSID with the saved SSID — reads event_type, status, reason; data not read; header fields are converted from big-endian in `cyw43_ll_parse_async_event` — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:348-354` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:223-232` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2335-2343` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:738-741`
- c-driver tests status == 0 and reason == 8 as literals; 8 is `CYW43_REASON_RSN_MISMATCH`. `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:349` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:117`
- whd — event receive path, before any handler: for `WLC_E_PRUNE` adds `WLC_E_PRUNE_REASON_OFFSET` (256) to the reason — reads event_type, reason — `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:872-875` `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:828-831` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:266`
- whd handlers therefore see prune reasons as received value + 256, while c-driver compares the unmodified reason (8); allow for the offset when comparing whd reason values with other drivers. `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:266` `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:874` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:349`
- Prune reason codes as c-driver defines them, `CYW43_REASON_PRUNE_` plus the name except 8, which is `CYW43_REASON_RSN_MISMATCH`: 1 ENCR_MISMATCH, 2 BCAST_BSSID, 3 MAC_DENY, 4 MAC_NA, 5 REG_PASSV, 6 SPCT_MGMT, 7 RADAR, 8 RSN_MISMATCH, 9 NO_COMMON_RATES, 10 BASIC_RATES, 11 CCXFAST_PREVAP, 12 CIPHER_NA, 13 KNOWN_STA, 14 CCXFAST_DROAM, 15 WDS_PEER, 16 QBSS_LOAD, 17 HOME_AP, 18 AP_BLOCKED, 19 NO_DIAG_SUPPORT. `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:110-128`
- whd defines the same list as `WLC_E_PRUNE_*` = n + 256 (RSN mismatch is `WLC_E_RSN_MISMATCH` = 8 + 256) and names them in `whd_reason_to_string`, but tests none. `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:308-326` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:315` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:438-456`
- linux, embassy — prune reason definitions: not found in these sources.
- c-driver's debug name table has no entry for PRUNE; traces print it as the number 23. `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:276-292` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:299-304`
- embassy — generic path only: converts the code with `as u8`, matches no link-state arm, and publishes `Status{event_type,status,reason}` only if the host-side mask has the event enabled, which no embassy code does for this event; the event is dropped after the debug log — reads event_type, status, flags, reason, auth_type in the match, data only logged — `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`
- whd — `case` label in `whd_wifi_join_events_handler` with no code of its own; it falls through to `default`, which only asserts ("Received event which was not registered"). The event is not in `join_events`, and the receive path calls a handler only for events in its registered list, so the label is not reached — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1497` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1477` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:906-925` `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:865`
- whd — `whd_event_to_string` lists it with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks, so the function returns "Unknown"; no caller of the function found in whd — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_utils.c:242` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:196-197` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:358` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:199` `whd/src/include/whd_utils.h:132`
- linux — no handler registered; an event without a handler is dropped in `brcmf_fweh_process_event` before it is queued — reads no fields — `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499`
- pico-sdk — no reference to this event: not found in these sources.

| Names | embassy `ADDTS_IND` `embassy/events.rs:72` / linux `BRCMF_E_ADDTS_IND` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:53` / whd `WLC_E_ADDTS_IND` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:72` (internal header only; no define in the public header `whd/inc/whd_events.h:35-58`) / c-driver: no `CYW43_EV_` define for this code `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:50-66`. Spelling after the prefix is identical in all drivers that define it. |
| Mask | embassy: enabled by default. The 24-byte mask starts all-ones, this event is not among the `unset()` calls, and the mask is sent as named variable `bsscfg:event_msgs` `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. Not enabled in the host-side event mask, which only gets SET_SSID/AUTH/PSK_SUP and ESCAN_RESULT `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`<br>c-driver: enabled by default. A 19-byte all-ones mask with only 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared is sent as `bsscfg:event_msgs` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`<br>linux: not enabled. The mask is zeroed and a bit is set only for events with a registered handler plus BRCMF_E_IF; no handler is registered for this event `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-462`. With the Cypress vendor ops the same mask bytes go out as named variable `event_msgs_ext` and `event_msgs` is skipped `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:79-99` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:459-460`<br>whd: left to the registered handler lists. The mask is zeroed and a bit set only for events in registered lists `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-416`; none of the driver's own lists contains this event `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70` `whd/src/COMPONENT_WLANSENSE/whd_wlansense_core.c:44-46`. An application can register for it `whd/src/whd_events.c:348` `whd/src/whd_events.c:443` |
| Handled by | no driver in these sources handles it `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` |
| Event data | no driver parses the data `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` |

- whd header comment: "indicate to host addts fail/success"; embassy carries the same doc comment. `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:72` `embassy/events.rs:71`
- embassy — generic path only: converts the code with `as u8`, matches no link-state arm, and publishes `Status{event_type,status,reason}` only if the host-side mask has the event enabled, which no embassy code does for this event; the event is dropped after the debug log — reads event_type, status, flags, reason, auth_type in the match, data only logged — `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`
- whd — `case` label in `whd_wifi_join_events_handler` with no code of its own; it falls through to `default`, which only asserts ("Received event which was not registered"). The event is not in `join_events`, and the receive path calls a handler only for events in its registered list, so the label is not reached — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1501` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1477` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:906-925` `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:865`
- whd — `whd_event_to_string` lists it with `CASE_RETURN`, which only breaks, so the function returns "Unknown"; no caller of the function found in whd — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_utils.c:246` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:196-197` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:358` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:199` `whd/src/include/whd_utils.h:132`
- linux — no handler registered; an event without a handler is dropped in `brcmf_fweh_process_event` before it is queued — reads no fields — `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499`
- c-driver — `cyw43_cb_process_async_event` has no branch for this event; the optional trace prints the code as a number — reads no fields — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:299-304`
- No status or reason value is tested by any driver. `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:498-499` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166`
- pico-sdk — no reference to this event: not found in these sources.

- whd header comment: "before attempting to roam"; embassy carries the same doc comment. `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:77` `embassy/events.rs:81`
- embassy — generic path only: converts the code with `as u8`, matches no link-state arm, and publishes `Status{event_type,status,reason}` only if the host-side mask has the event enabled, which no embassy code does for this event; the event is dropped after the debug log — reads event_type, status, flags, reason, auth_type in the match, data only logged — `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`
- whd — `case` label in `whd_wifi_join_events_handler` with no code of its own; it falls through to `default`, which only asserts ("Received event which was not registered"). - c-driver — `cyw43_cb_process_async_event` has no branch for this event; the optional trace prints the code as a number — reads no fields — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:299-304`

Spelling after the prefix is identical in all drivers that define it. |
| Mask | embassy: enabled by default. The 24-byte mask starts all-ones, this event is not among the `unset()` calls, and the mask is sent as named variable `bsscfg:event_msgs` `embassy/control.rs:305-319`. Not enabled in the host-side event mask, which only gets SET_SSID/AUTH/PSK_SUP and ESCAN_RESULT `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`<br>c-driver: enabled by default. A 19-byte all-ones mask with only 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared is sent as `bsscfg:event_msgs` `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`<br>linux: enabled. A handler is registered `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:7011` and every event with a handler gets its mask bit, sent as named variable `event_msgs` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-462`. With the Cypress vendor ops the same mask bytes go out as named variable `event_msgs_ext` and `event_msgs` is skipped `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:79-99` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:459-460`<br>whd: left to the registered handler lists. The mask is zeroed and a bit set only for events in registered lists `whd/src/whd_events.c:409-416`; none of the driver's own lists contains this event `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70` `whd/src/COMPONENT_WLANSENSE/whd_wlansense_core.c:44-46`. An application can register for it `whd/src/whd_events.c:348` `whd/src/whd_events.c:443` |
| Handled by | linux (`brcmf_notify_sched_scan_results`; `brcmf_wowl_nd_results` while net-detect is armed) `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3910-3992` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4085-4131`. embassy, whd, c-driver: no handler `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354`. |
| Event data | linux only: header `struct brcmf_pno_scanresults_le`, little-endian, 12 bytes: version u32 @0, status u32 @4, count u32 @8 `linux/brcmfmac/fwil_types.h:1072-1076`. If version == 2 the header is `struct brcmf_pno_scanresults_v2_le`, 16 bytes: the same three fields plus scan_ch_bucket u32 @12 `linux/brcmfmac/fwil_types.h:1078-1083`. Version 1, and any other value with a WARN, uses the 12-byte header `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3887-3898`. Then `count` entries of `struct brcmf_pno_net_info_le`, 44 bytes each: bssid u8[6] @0, channel u8 @6 (channel number only), SSID_len u8 @7, SSID u8[32] @8, RSSI le16 @40 (dBm), timestamp le16 @42 (age in seconds) `linux/brcmfmac/fwil_types.h:1046-1063`. The structs have no packed attribute; with natural alignment the fields above leave no padding, giving 12, 16 and 44 bytes (computed) `linux/brcmfmac/fwil_types.h:1056-1063` `linux/brcmfmac/fwil_types.h:1072-1083` |

- whd header comment: "PFN network found event"; embassy carries the same doc comment. `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:78` `embassy/events.rs:83`
- linux — `brcmf_notify_sched_scan_results`: checks the data length, reads count and status from the result header, adds each entry's SSID and channel to an internal escan request, looks up the matching sched-scan bucket, then starts a targeted escan; on error reports the sched scan as stopped — reads datalen, event_code; data: version, status, count, per entry SSID, SSID_len, channel, and bssid/SSID in `brcmf_pno_get_bucket_map`; header status and reason not read — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3910-3992` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3969-3977` `linux/brcmfmac/pno.c:565-595` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:7011`
- linux — `brcmf_wowl_nd_results`: swapped in as the PFN_NET_FOUND handler while wake-on-WLAN net-detect is armed, restored afterwards; stores the first entry's SSID and channel (as a frequency) as the net-detect match and wakes the resume task — reads datalen, event_code; data: count, version (to locate the first entry), first entry's SSID_len, SSID, channel; header status and reason not read — `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4085-4131` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3887-3898` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4277-4280` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4231-4233`
- linux tests values inside the data, not the event header. Data status is expected to be `BRCMF_PNO_SCAN_COMPLETE` (1), otherwise `WARN_ON` only. `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3944` `linux/brcmfmac/pno.h:8`
- linux rejects count == 0 ("FALSE PNO Event"); the wowl path returns -EINVAL for count < 1. `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3946-3949` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4107-4111`
- linux size checks: datalen >= 12 + 44, and remaining datalen >= count * 44. SSID_len is clamped to 32. `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3927` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4095` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3952-3953` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3969-3970` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4114-4115`
- linux expects that one result set may arrive as several NET_FOUND events (comment: the event is limited to 512 bytes). `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3941-3943`
- No driver tests the event header status or reason. `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:3910-3992` `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:4085-4131` `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1347` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-354` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166`

The event is not in `join_events`, and the receive path calls a handler only for events in its registered list, so the label is not reached — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1507` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1477` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` `whd/src/whd_cdc_bdc.c:906-925` `whd/src/whd_msgbuf_txrx.c:865`
- whd — `whd_event_to_string` returns the string "WLC_E_PFN_NET_FOUND" (debug naming); no caller of the function found in whd — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_utils.c:251` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:193-194` `whd/src/whd_utils.c:199` `whd/src/include/whd_utils.h:132`

### 36 JOIN_START
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | embassy `JOIN_START` `embassy/events.rs:88` / linux `BRCMF_E_JOIN_START` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:62` / whd `WLC_E_JOIN_START` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:80` / c-driver: not found `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:50-66`. Same spelling apart from the prefix. |
| Mask | embassy: default mask. The `bsscfg:event_msgs` mask sent at init is 24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG and ROAM cleared; this bit stays set `embassy/control.rs:305-319`<br>c-driver: default mask. 19 mask bytes of 0xff with only bits 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared; this bit stays set `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`<br>linux: not enabled. The mask is zeroed and only events with a registered handler, plus IF, get their bit `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-456`; no `brcmf_fweh_register` call for this event found in linux. Until `brcmf_fweh_activate_events` runs (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:8637`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:8674`), preinit has only read the firmware's own `event_msgs` mask, set the IF bit and written it back; these sources do not show that firmware default `linux/brcmfmac/common.c:414-429`. The mask goes out as the named variable (iovar) `event_msgs`, or as `event_msgs_ext` through the cyw vendor hook `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:458-463` `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:79-99`<br>whd: not enabled by default. The mask is zeroed and only events in a registered handler list are set `whd/src/whd_events.c:408-419`; this event is in none of the internal lists `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70` `whd/src/COMPONENT_WLANSENSE/whd_wlansense_core.c:44-46`; an application can enable any code by passing its own list to `whd_wifi_set_event_handler`, which re-sends the mask `whd/src/whd_events.c:443-492` `whd/inc/whd_events.h:230` |
| Handled by | no driver in these sources handles it `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-420` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1357-1616` |
| Event data | no driver parses the data `embassy/runner.rs:1237-1294` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-420` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1357-1616` |

### 37 ROAM_START
| | |
|---|---|
| Names | embassy `ROAM_START` `embassy/events.rs:89` / linux `BRCMF_E_ROAM_START` `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:63` / whd `WLC_E_ROAM_START` `whd/src/include/whd_events_int.h:80` / c-driver: not found `c-driver/cyw43_ll.h:50-66`. Same spelling apart from the prefix. |
| Mask | embassy: default mask. The `bsscfg:event_msgs` mask sent at init is 24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG and ROAM cleared; this bit stays set `embassy/control.rs:305-319`<br>c-driver: default mask. 19 mask bytes of 0xff with only bits 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared; this bit stays set `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`<br>linux: not enabled. The mask is zeroed and only events with a registered handler, plus IF, get their bit `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:444-456`; no `brcmf_fweh_register` call for this event found in linux. Until `brcmf_fweh_activate_events` runs (`linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:8637`, `linux/brcmfmac/cfg80211.c:8674`), preinit has only read the firmware's own `event_msgs` mask, set the IF bit and written it back; these sources do not show that firmware default `linux/brcmfmac/common.c:414-429`. The mask goes out as the named variable `event_msgs`, or as `event_msgs_ext` through the cyw vendor hook `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:458-463` `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:79-99`<br>whd: not enabled by default. The mask is zeroed and only events in a registered handler list are set `whd/src/whd_events.c:408-419`; this event is in none of the internal lists `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-166` `whd/src/whd_ap.c:70` `whd/src/COMPONENT_WLANSENSE/whd_wlansense_core.c:44-46`; an application can enable any code by passing its own list to `whd_wifi_set_event_handler`, which re-sends the mask `whd/src/whd_events.c:443-492` `whd/inc/whd_events.h:230` |

- linux — `brcmf_psm_watchdog_notify`: logs that the PSM watchdog fired and creates a device coredump with `brcmf_debug_create_memdump`, which copies the event data to the front of the buffer and appends a RAM dump of the chip — reads data (opaque, copied verbatim) and datalen; no status, reason, flags or addr — `linux/brcmfmac/core.c:978-995` `linux/brcmfmac/debug.c:17-43`
- embassy — no event-specific handling: debug-logs the event, leaves link state alone (`_ => false` arm), and publishes it to the event queue only if the driver-side mask has it enabled; embassy enables only SET_SSID/AUTH/PSK_SUP and ESCAN_RESULT there — tests no message fields; a published message would carry event_type, status and reason with `Payload::None` — `embassy/runner.rs:1228-1235` `embassy/runner.rs:1293` `embassy/runner.rs:1316-1345` `embassy/control.rs:464` `embassy/control.rs:828`
- c-driver — no handling: `cyw43_cb_process_async_event` has no branch for this event type; dumped only when `CYW43_TRACE_ASYNC_EV` is set — reads no fields — `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:312-420` `c-driver/cyw43_ctrl.c:315-317`
- whd — no handling: a case label in `whd_wifi_join_events_handler`, in the block listed for gcc pedantic checking; the block falls through to `default`, which only calls `whd_assert(..., 0 != 0)` and breaks. That handler is registered for `join_events` only, which does not list this event — reads no fields — `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1515` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1477` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:1613-1615` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:156-160` `whd/src/whd_wifi_api.c:2069`
- No driver tests status, reason or flags for this event; linux reads only data and datalen `linux/brcmfmac/core.c:989-990`

| | |

Same spelling apart from the prefix. |
| Mask | embassy: default mask. The `bsscfg:event_msgs` mask sent at init is 24 bytes of 0xFF with only RADIO, IF, PROBREQ_MSG, PROBREQ_MSG_RX, PROBRESP_MSG and ROAM cleared; this bit stays set `embassy/control.rs:305-319`<br>c-driver: default mask. 19 mask bytes of 0xff with only bits 20, 40, 44, 54, 71 cleared; this bit stays set `c-driver/cyw43_ll.c:2051-2060`<br>linux: event code not defined, so linux cannot register a handler or set the bit itself `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.h:62-76`. The mask goes out as the named variable `event_msgs`, or as `event_msgs_ext` through the cyw vendor hook `linux/brcmfmac/fweh.c:458-463` `linux/brcmfmac/cyw/core.c:79-99`<br>whd: not enabled by default.
